Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the stratigraphy object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 52. The moduli space has dimension at most 524 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by stratigraphy generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 52, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 524. QED.

Theorem 216.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)
